Existing Apple M5 Max profiles identify real hot work: string/Unicode materialization dominates several direct paths; exact number materialization dominates numeric corpora; object-heavy ASCII inputs lose badly to simdjson’s staged classifier; an earlier event-cursor prototype regressed on all six measured corpora. Those facts rule out prematurely favoring either event tapes or one universal SIMD stage. I’m forming the twelve-carrier tape portfolio around these measured failure modes.
````

## Exact campaign state

| Plane | Implemented or formed | Still required |
|---|---|---|
| SK-v25 foundation | Model-truth gate, evidence census, re-adjudication protocol, lock constitution, tranche formation | Commit and reconcile the later campaign corpus; correct stale status surfaces |
| Parse T | Eight detailed wave specifications; V1–V3 falsifiers; V4 isolated control; PT-0 harness | Production T.W0–W7, package transpose, consumers, two clean hostile passes |
| Parse U | Ten detailed specifications; unsealed U.W0 witness bank; partial dirty ARM-only work | U.W0 seal, safe kernel, one scanner, regex HIR/VM, bootstrap, TS/Rust/Wasm parity, Value adoption, gestalts |
| Parse engine assays | Five designs specified | **0/5 implemented** |
| Structure DAG | Substantial graph exporter; focused replay 80 passed, 12 campaign gates ignored | P3.1 is still RED, clean count 0/2; cluster-directed hostile loop and movement authority remain |
| BBNF A–E | Thirty-five obligation waves authored | Product implementation; these waves are no longer valid as an assumed linear landing order |
| Type prototypes | V1–V4 produced useful counterexamples | All are falsified, retired, or incomplete; no accepted generalized type plane |
| JSON/CSS assays | Large external corpora, controls, inventories and adversarial evidence | Normative closure, full semantics, candidate integration, two clean passes |
| VT portfolio | Four independent architectures precisely specified | **0/4 implemented** |
| Backend/value/tape | Requirements formed | Exact BBNF-derived Rust/TS types, generalized API, tape, modular IR backends and deep ARM generation |
| Performance | Floors specified | No eligible candidate: CSS ≥5× Lightning CSS and JSON ≥1.1× sonic-rs remain unproven |
| Playground | Frontend research and a few provisional deletions | Glass UI 7.0.0, structural transpose, every-page visual/accessibility/performance assays |
| Release | Published parse-that 1.0.0 remains green as the control | No new releasable dual-repository tranche |

The controlling documents are [HANDOFF.md](/Users/mkbabb/Programming/bbnf-lang/restart/skinny/tranches/sk-v25/HANDOFF.md), [LOCK-CONSTITUTION.md](/Users/mkbabb/Programming/bbnf-lang/restart/skinny/tranches/sk-v25/LOCK-CONSTITUTION.md), [CHALLENGE-LEDGER.md](/Users/mkbabb/Programming/bbnf-lang/restart/skinny/tranches/sk-v25/CHALLENGE-LEDGER.md), and the superseding [BB-VALUE-TAPE-P0.md](/Users/mkbabb/Programming/bbnf-lang/restart/skinny/tranches/sk-v25/waves/BB-VALUE-TAPE-P0.md).

## Passes 1–3, Alpha, and Omega

There are two similarly named pass systems; conflating them has overstated progress.

- Historical design passes: old BBNF reached pass 2 and parse-that reached pass 3. Their machine evidence survives, but their judgments are tainted by the SK-v25 model-truth finding. Pending BBNF pass 3 and parse pass 4 were folded into apotheosis.
- Current graph P1/P2/P3: all three were implemented and author-reproduced, then hostile-falsified and superseded. P3.1 preserves useful graph machinery but remains RED with movement forbidden.
- Alpha: SK-v23/v24 `PASS-ALPHA` was tranche-development research—not product implementation. There is no accepted current SK-v25 Alpha closure.
- Omega: no SK-v25 Omega has run. Omega1 and Omega2 remain pending, as do GESTALT-P1, P2, B1 and B2.

The original SK-v25 commit `af15f63e0` is therefore doing exactly what it claims: it is a handoff and re-adjudication charter. Only `HANDOFF.md`, `INVENTORY.md`, and `PROMPTS.md` are committed there; the subsequent campaign corpus and implementation experiments remain dirty/untracked.

## Current executable truth

Parse-that:

- Published TypeScript 1.0.0: **124/124 tests green**.
- PT-0 harness: **16/16 local tests**, but verification is RED because the pin receipt drifted.
- Rust T/U work: locked compilation cannot begin because `Cargo.lock` would change.
- U.W0: 52 born-RED tests exist but were not sealed against pristine bytes before accompanying production edits.
- PT-E/J/C/K/R/V: all RED, hostile clean count 0/2.
- Partial x86 removal exists, but repository-wide ARM-only closure is unproved.

BBNF:

- P3.1 movement authority: RED.
- Type V4: tests may pass locally, but adjudicated `INCOMPLETE_ASSAY`; no candidate credit.
- JSON Family A: locally functioning narrow assay, formally retired as incomplete.
- CSS and JSON banks: external evidence only.
- Four complete value/tape candidates: 0/4.
- Full CSS L4 and JSON semantics: not implemented.
- Exact grammar-derived type/value/backend system: not implemented.

## Value boundary

The latest Value checkpoint is accepted exactly as coordination evidence:

- No parse-that freeze remains.
- Value’s sealed formation epoch was not mutated.
- No authority transferred.
- `@mkbabb/parse-that@1.0.0` remains its sole consumable parser.
- PT-E/P00/P01 still require fresh public snapshots.
- U.W0 and later adoption work require a future bounded upstream window.

Value’s convergence improves the consumer specification; it does not advance parse-that or BBNF implementation completion.

## Ordered remainder

1. Reconcile dirty ownership, commit or discard evidence deliberately, and supersede stale campaign rows.
2. Seal PT-0/U.W0 on controlled bytes; run two hostile critics and adjudication.
3. Perfect—not greenfield-replace—parse-that through T.W1–W7 and U.W1–W7.
4. Close parse GESTALT-P1/P2 twice and publish the candidate needed by Value’s fresh-snapshot gates.
5. Finish graph P3.1 sufficiently to authorize cluster-directed modularization.

7. Execute VT.W0, including atomic coverage ledgers for every PT/BB/original/XL/tail lock.
8. Independently implement VT-1…VT-4 with no shared compiler, solver, runtime, regex engine, or test helper.
9. Profile and challenge each through two clean total-tranche, wave, feature, and gestalt passes.
10. Select at VT.W6; transpose atomically; delete superseded production paths without aliases or shims.
11. Close B1/B2, replay P1/P2, then close Omega1/Omega2 twice.
12. Only then perform the packed Glass UI 7.0.0 playground uplift and exhaustive visual verification.

The proper next execution point is therefore **authority reconciliation followed by PT-0/U.W0 closure**—not VT implementation, backend expansion, or frontend work yet.

Hic et ubique: no implemented wave will henceforth be called complete without two hostile gestalt passes over the same corrected bytes, including tranche fit, wave fidelity, feature truth, KISS, and greater-product effect.

| BBNF BB-A…E specifications | All 35 obligation waves are formed | 35/35 specs |
| Full every-lock VT implementations | No complete vertical; earlier type kernels and JSON Family A/D were falsified or retired | 0/4 |
| Product gestalts | Historical Alpha/pass-1/2/3 evidence exists, but none is a current P1/P2/B1/B2/omega close | 0/6 |
| Full-parity competitive results | Many benches and narrow probes exist | 0 qualifying CSS or JSON results |

What has genuinely landed and survived challenge in the current architecture campaign is narrower: the R14/R15 graph-validation repair corrected the bounded-cost formula to `G = 4T + 9N + 19E`; R15 is frozen static-green and its isolated Apple-ARM replay later passed 23/23. That is graph/evidence infrastructure, not parser convergence. R16-P2’s Apple capability-launch implementation was rejected after both hostile reviews. R16-P3 is now authorized as the smaller forward rework; at the latest byte check its implementation files had not yet changed.

For parse-that, the current structure is:

```text
TypeScript 1.0.0
Parser definition + mutable ParserState
  → leaf/combinator methods
  → opt-in packrat/LR state
  → JSON/CSV domain parsers
  → ESM/CJS package faces

Rust 0.4.0
ParserState + Parser/leaf/combinators
  ├─ legacy SpanParser hierarchy
  ├─ scanner families and architecture-specific kernels
  ├─ handwritten JSON/CSV/CSS parsers
  └─ embedded generated-regex host

bbnf-regex 0.1.0
handwritten regex syntax/HIR
  → algebra/egraph analysis
  → NFA → DFA
  → classification/sets/UTF-8
```

That is a real and substantial parser library, not a greenfield shell. The published TS package is `@mkbabb/parse-that@1.0.0`; the sibling Rust crate is `parse_that 0.4.0`. The private tree at `ef10d5b…` is heavily dirty and remains Claude-owned.

Its principal unresolved structure defects are concrete:

- T’s target immutable definition graph and invocation-owned run context have not replaced the current TS runtime. V1–V3 were falsifiers; V4 is only an isolated control, not production.
- U is explicitly `FORMED; born RED`. `ParserState`, `SpanParser`, `LazyParser`, `BumpSlab`, public/raw span-state seams, duplicated scanner routes, and the handwritten CSS route remain.
- The measured PT-K map finds 136 `unsafe` occurrences in 20 production files and 24 x86/SSE/AVX/PCLMUL vocabulary matches. Therefore the Apple-ARM-only lock is not closed.
- The separate regex engine has useful HIR, algebra, egraph, NFA, and DFA organs, but the canonical syntax/HIR/VM/capture/bootstrap contract required by PT-R is not complete.
- Tests are mostly external, but U’s required isomorphic `run/value/scanner/regex` topology and the full witness matrix are not sealed.
- PT-C Rust/TS/Wasm isomorphism, PT-V adoption, and both parse gestalts remain open.

For bbnf-lang, two parser/compiler lines coexist:

```text
Root production line
BBNF module
  → generated self-hosted BbnfBootstrap using parse_that
  → tape/document/view
  → host extraction into AST + directives
  → mutable GrammarIR
  → lowering + recognizers + egraph/CSP/type/layout passes
  → Rust / TypeScript / Wasm / VM backends
  → generated grammar-specific runtimes

Skinny experimental line
handwritten BBNF parser
  → compact GrammarIr arena
  → passes::compile
  → PipelineOutput
  → codegen
  → checked-in generated runtime
```

The root line is functional and materially developed: a canonical checked-in self-host parser, nine manifest grammars, Rust/TS/Wasm plus VM targets, tape/document/value views, egraph and CSP substrates, and broad external tests. Generated parser sizes alone include roughly 21,120 lines for BBNF, 3,320 for JSON, and 108,317 for CSS L4. Those are implementation facts, not full-L4 proof.

The BBNF structure is still far from the requested end state:

- `GrammarIR` is a mutable 32-public-field pass-state bag with many analysis sidecars. It is not the sealed, modular, backend-neutral program spine required by BB-B/BB-C.
- The current type solver is not generalized Rust-grade inference: `TypeDomain` is effectively `Option<TypeDesc>` and can replace unequal assignments. Skinny’s “infer” is structural classification, not unification or a generalized L4 typing API.
- Root and skinny are duplicate product planes. Selection, atomic transposition, and deletion of the loser have not happened.
- JSON, CSS, Sheets, runtime, and emitter specialization remain scattered. Skinny also contains byte-identical checked-in template/runtime copies.
- The source topology is not yet compliant: root still has inline tests; skinny has roughly 433 inline test functions versus 67 external, and most root core test files remain a flat pile rather than a source-isomorphic tree.
- Several large modules remain—skinny grammar and passes are each about 2.1k lines; its benchmark report is about 11.8k—and the root observational grammar API intentionally leaks its source and document.
- Fifteen backend default arms still say prettify is unsupported. Exact grammar-derived Rust/TS L4 types, full value/tape semantics, generalized IR backends, deep ARM kernels, full JSON/CSS conformance, packaging, frontend transposition, and losing-path deletion remain unclosed.
- bbnf-lang currently path-patches directly to the dirty sibling Rust parse-that crates. Thus its parser substrate is live/private, not a sealed immutable parse-that input.

The immediate ordered remainder is therefore:

1. Finish and twice challenge the small R16-P3 architecture-evidence rework.
2. Let the Claude-owned parse-that campaign seal T, then U, and both parse gestalts; Codex does not mutate that tree.
3. Re-extract and close the BBNF typed structure graph until it authorizes product movement.
4. Freeze exhaustive JSON and CSS L4 semantic corpora plus Apple-ARM benchmark controls.
5. Build four independent full every-lock VT verticals—not four partial kernels.
6. Select by conformance and profiling, transpose once, delete all losing/legacy paths, then close B1/B2 and replay P1/P2.
7. Only then perform the Glass UI 7 playground plane and the two omega gestalts.

The standing law is ratified hic et ubique: implementation does not become accepted because it compiles or passes its author tests. Every wave receives two hostile tranche/wave/feature gestalt challenges and a fresh adjudication; any sustained defect resets its clean-pass count.
````

## 1339 — 2026-07-22T03:19:03.227Z

- Body SHA-256: `a2f6efe3ed093d717e3baa5f052df7066457b92e4595a4855d0fe072dc334488`
- UTF-8 bytes: 6377

````
The honest status: formation is mature; the final product is not close to convergence. We are close only on one narrow BBNF architecture-evidence repair. Most work hitherto has established specifications, falsified incomplete designs, and built trustworthy graph/evidence machinery—not accepted parser/compiler waves.

## What the project is

One grammar-driven system:

`BBNF modules → parse/regex → inferred types → immutable IR → CSP/e-graph/cost solving → Rust/TS/Wasm/Apple-ARM backends → hybrid value/tape API → exhaustive JSON/CSS L4 → Glass UI playground`

No x86, handwritten semantic sidecars, legacy routes, aliases, or benchmark-specific overfitting.

## Exact standing

| Plane | Current truth |
|---|---|
| SK-v25 authority | Goal active; model-truth gate established permanently |
| Formation | parse-that T/U: **18/18 specs**; BBNF BB-A…E: **35/35 specs** |
| Accepted parse-that waves | **0/18** |
| Complete BBNF/value candidates | **0/4** |
| Product gestalts | **0/6** — P1, P2, B1, B2, omega1, omega2 all open |
| Full-parity competitive results | **0** |
| Glass UI 7 playground | Not started beyond provisional deletion/structure work |
| Repository state | BBNF remains at `af15f63e`; no campaign commit or staged changes |

This is recorded in [CAMPAIGN-STATE.md](/Users/mkbabb/Programming/bbnf-lang/restart/skinny/tranches/sk-v25/CAMPAIGN-STATE.md) and the binding [VT portfolio](/Users/mkbabb/Programming/bbnf-lang/restart/skinny/tranches/sk-v25/waves/BB-VALUE-TAPE-P0.md).

## What has actually been implemented

- The SK-v25 handoff, lock constitution, evidence census, tranche portfolio, challenge ledger, conformance banks, and all 35 BB-A…E wave specifications exist.
- Typed architecture extraction now exists under [xtask/src/architecture](/Users/mkbabb/Programming/bbnf-lang/xtask/src/architecture): Cargo/Rust/frontend/artifact collectors, typed graph/schema, SCCs, cycles, quotient/cluster projections, receipts, transactions, and evidence validation.
- Graph P1, P2, and P3 were implemented and then correctly falsified. P3.1 retained useful graph machinery but still has **0/2 accepted structural passes**.
- Type-kernel V1–V4 and JSON vertical A were implemented as assays. Hostile review proved them incomplete or structurally wrong; none may donate product code or claim VT completion.
- JSON and CSS conformance machinery has become considerably stronger, but both remain RED. JSON has substantial two-root oracle and adversarial-number evidence; CSS has pinned source/WPT work and repeated hostile findings. Neither proves full semantics.
- Apple-only architecture enforcement has removed active x86 paths from the current BBNF delta and introduced an explicit Apple capability-launch boundary.
- The immediate R16-P7 change reduced the Apple evidence implementation to its adjudicated line budgets without changing semantics. Critic A found no product defect. Blind Critic B is still working and presently also tends static-GREEN; fresh adjudication remains mandatory.
- R14/R15 corrected the bounded graph validation cost formula and passed the isolated 23/23 runtime assay. That is evidence infrastructure, not parser closure.

The BBNF working tree currently contains 99 tracked-file changes totaling roughly **+1,566/−4,572**, plus extensive untracked campaign material. Some changes are protected concurrent-owner work, so this diff cannot be treated wholesale as ours or accepted.

## Parser structure today

parse-that is an existing system being perfected—not replaced:

- TypeScript combinator/parser kernel: [typescript/src/parse](/Users/mkbabb/Programming/parse-that/typescript/src/parse)
- Rust combinators, scanners, JSON/CSS parsers, and remaining legacy `SpanParser`: [rust/parse_that/src](/Users/mkbabb/Programming/parse-that/rust/parse_that/src)
- Regex syntax/HIR, algebra, e-graph, NFA/DFA: [rust/regex/src](/Users/mkbabb/Programming/parse-that/rust/regex/src)
- BBNF regex bootstrap: [rust/bootstrap](/Users/mkbabb/Programming/parse-that/rust/bootstrap)

Its T.W0–W7 and U.W0–W9 specifications exist, but none is accepted. Claude Code owns that tree under [CLAUDE-PROOF-HANDOFF.md](/Users/mkbabb/Programming/parse-that/docs/tranches/U/CLAUDE-PROOF-HANDOFF.md); Codex will not mutate or contact it until handback.

BBNF currently has two competing architectural strata:

- Root: generated BBNF parser/runtime, mutable grammar/pipeline/lowering/generation machinery under [crates/core/src](/Users/mkbabb/Programming/bbnf-lang/crates/core/src).
- Skinny: compact experimental IR, passes, codegen, runtime, regex, and benchmark carriers under [skinny/crates](/Users/mkbabb/Programming/bbnf-lang/skinny/crates).

That duality remains unresolved. The current parser is functional, but it is not yet the demanded grammar-only, immutable, fully inferred, backend-modular product. Root/skinny duplication, mutable IR sidecars, generated-runtime breadth, module granularity, and test topology still require final disposition.

## What remains

1. Finish R16-P7’s blind review and adjudication.
2. Separately prove the physical Cargo/rustc/link launch route on Apple ARM; the prior signal-9 linker death remains causally unproved.
3. Close P3.1 with two consecutive clean hostile passes, eliminating the ratified **234 unknown facts across 438 obligations** before structural movement.
4. Complete and twice challenge parse-that T.W0–W7 and U.W0–W9; close P1/P2.
5. Freeze truly exhaustive JSON and CSS-L4 semantic matrices and matched benchmark controls.
6. Build four independent, non-sharing every-lock VT implementations—each with grammar-derived exact Rust/TS types, full value/tape APIs, JSON, CSS L4, Rust/TS/Wasm/Apple-ARM backends, profiles, packages, and consumers.
7. Select by complete semantics and measured cost, then transpose atomically and delete the losing/root/skinny/legacy routes.
8. Prove at least 5× Lightning CSS with full parity, targeting 6×; and at least 1.1× sonic-rs, targeting 2×.
9. Close B1/B2, replay P1/P2, perform the packed Glass UI 7.0.0 playground uplift and visual assays, then close omega1/omega2.

So: one narrow infrastructure wave is nearing closure; the perfected parse-that/BBNF union is not. Hic et ubique, no implemented wave earns acceptance until two fresh hostile passes and adjudication are clean on identical corrected bytes.
````

## What has actually been done

### Campaign and architecture formation

- The SK-v25 handoff, inventory, prompts, evidence census, apotheosis, lock constitution, and ordered tranche portfolio exist.
- The permanent model-truth gate is established.
- Six parse locks, four BBNF locks, structural graph laws, hostile-pass laws, and all six final gestalts are specified.
- Four orthogonal VT designs are fully chartered:
  - VT-1 staged compiler/compact tape
  - VT-2 verified modules/schema row stores
  - VT-3 persistent incremental compiler/rope tape
  - VT-4 GLL/SPPF/eager typed arena
- The CSS module reset DAG received terminal shared-boundary ACK at SHA-256 `291e5145…`: all 15 trunks reachable, no cycles, unique semantic owners, primitive-only tokens, and exact named-range keyframe selector laws. This is coordination evidence, not production implementation.

### Parse-that

Parse-that is not greenfield:

- Published TypeScript `@mkbabb/parse-that@1.0.0` exists and remains Value’s only consumable parser.
- Rust `0.4.0` exists as the incumbent control.
- T.W0–T.W7 and U.W0–U.W9 are fully specified.
- Invocation prototypes V1–V4 exist:
  - V1–V3 were hostile-falsified.
  - V4 is a strong isolated control with recorded `24/24` normal and `4/4` extended tests, 1.65 million oracle comparisons, public package checks, and stable artifact hashes.
  - V4 remains unaccepted and untransposed.
- PT-0’s evidence harness recorded 16/16 tests, but its receipt drifted and selected toolchain provenance remains unproved.
- U.W0 has a 55-test preseal bank: 33 controls/already-satisfied cases and 22 intended RED defects.
- Six Rust product files currently contain an Apple-ARM/Wasm scanner overlay, net `+190/−118`. It removes x86 number/PCLMUL directions and adds NEON/Wasm/SWAR and `ByteClass8` work.

But none of this closes a T/U production wave. The current [Claude handoff](/Users/mkbabb/Programming/parse-that/docs/tranches/U/CLAUDE-PROOF-HANDOFF.md:3) explicitly calls U.W0 preseal research.

### BBNF-lang

A great deal of implementation evidence exists:

- Graph P3.1 contains 29 new source files/~17,055 lines and 24 modular test files/~13,576 lines.
- Its static census contains 183 tests, 12 ignored.
- One exact graph correction is genuinely accepted: the P7 one-file `−27` line/`−414` byte representation change, receipt `23822b68…`.
- Type-kernel V1–V4 prototypes exist and produced useful counterexamples.
- JSON Family A exists and produced useful exact-source/SCC evidence.
- CSS and JSON conformance banks are large, executable assays.
- The dirty tracked BBNF work spans 99 files, net `+1,566/−4,572`, including:
  - generated grammar reduction;
  - runtime/tape/value changes;
  - architecture exporter machinery;
  - ARM/NEON/Wasm SIMD work;
  - deletion of AVX2/AVX512 sources;
  - CSS diagnostics and typed-runtime work;
  - provisional playground simplification.

However:

- P1/P2/P3 were hostile-falsified.
- P3.1 still has 234 `Unknown` facts among 438 required graph obligations and `0/2` hostile-clean passes.
- P8 closed `BLOCKED / NO ACCEPTED OBSERVATION / NO CARGO`.
- Type kernels V1–V4 are retired as incomplete horizontal assays.
- JSON Family A was retired as an incomplete assay; Family D never started.
- None of the four canonical `prototypes/value-tape/vt-{1,2,3,4}` roots exists.
- None of the broad runtime/generated/SIMD changes has accepted SK-v25 product credit.
- Much of the work overlaps protected concurrent-owner changes and is unstaged.

## Conformance status

CSS:

- Epoch 5 contains substantial sealed source/WPT inventory machinery.
- Two critics found 20 total valid defects.
- Adjudication is RED, clean count zero.
- No full CSS L4, WPT, package, candidate, or production credit exists.

JSON:

- The latest corrected oracle has strong recorded evidence: two-root 47-test replay, 318 suite rows, 104 + 100,000 number checks, and hostile 95–96 MiB resource envelopes.
- Gestalt-5 critic A is RED on launcher provenance.
- Critic B and adjudication are absent.
- Clean count remains zero.

No benchmark currently proves full-parity ≥5× Lightning CSS or ≥1.1× sonic-rs. The aspirational targets remain 6× and 2×.

## What remains

The exact critical path is:

1. Parse-that T:
   - T.W0 artifact truth
   - invocation kernel
   - combinator/result algebra
   - diagnostics/recovery
   - memoization/LR
   - package/source DAG
   - consumer/coordinate truth
   - two clean hostile audits and `GESTALT-T`

2. Parse-that U:
   - recapture U.W0 in a clean detached crater
   - safe checked Rust kernel
   - canonical Apple ARM scanner contract
   - regex syntax/HIR and bounded ordered VM
   - deterministic bootstrap/fixed point
   - TS/Rust/Wasm crossing
   - packed Value adoption proof
   - GESTALT-P1 and fresh GESTALT-P2

3. Explicit Claude handback with exact accepted byte epoch, receipts, lock ledger, and dirty-file ownership.

4. Complete the BBNF structural DAG:
   - resolve all 438 obligations;
   - assign every owner;
   - perform module/test transposition;
   - obtain two consecutive hostile-clean passes and movement authority.

5. Freeze exhaustive CSS L4 and JSON semantics, controls, normative ledgers, and Apple ARM benchmark identities.

6. Implement all four independent VT candidates in full. Each must independently contain every PT/BB/historical/XL/tail lock, exact grammar-derived Rust/TS types, generalized value/tape API, CSS/JSON, Rust/TS/Wasm/ARM backends, packages, consumers, profiles, and private evidence.

7. Profile, run unseen holdouts, and challenge every candidate twice.

8. Select one winner, transpose it atomically, and delete all losing/legacy paths without aliases, shims, fallbacks, or dual routes.

9. Close B1/B2, replay P1/P2, then close omega1/omega2.

10. Finally land packed Glass UI 7.0.0 and perform every-page visual, interaction, accessibility, and performance verification. The current playground still uses Glass UI 3.0.0.

Historical `PASS-ALPHA`, Parse passes 1–3, and BBNF passes 1–2 are evidence banks, not current completion. The real product gestalts have not run.

The goal is presently blocked only because Claude still owns parse-that and no handback exists—not because the repositories lack substantial work.

## What the original audit actually completed

Completed:

- The model-truth revelation was proved and converted into a permanent gate.

- The four non-sharing full verticals were specified.

Not completed:

- The original requirement to re-adjudicate every old registry/work item until two consecutive clean passes.
- Three complete SK-v25 design passes over every lock.
- Any product-level Alpha, parse gestalt, BBNF gestalt, or Omega.

The old SK-v24 `PASS-ALPHA`, BBNF passes 1–2, and parse passes 1–3 are historical, model-tainted formation inputs. The newer graph P1/P2/P3 are a different sequence; all were hostile-falsified. Neither series counts as `GESTALT-P1/P2`, `GESTALT-B1/B2`, or Omega.

## Addenda status

## Parse-that

The existing parser has not been greenfield-replaced:

- HEAD: `ef10d5b78236c4a30a7bb22a6113b60bdc4bdf42`
- Published package: `@mkbabb/parse-that@1.0.0`
- Published commit: `7eab78c89961001a689952c091fdbbf64af735da`

The pre-SK-v25 baseline genuinely contains the A→B→Q→S work: CSS parser removal, packrat repairs, SpanParser/legacy-span removal, the armed packrat latch, and the 1.0.0 clean break. Those are real prior achievements—not the perfected SK-v25 result.

Current SK-v25 status:

- Invocation v2: implemented, falsified.
- Invocation v3: implemented, falsified.
- Invocation v4: complete isolated control carrier with useful executable evidence, but no hostile acceptance or production transposition.
- PT-0 R3–R6: repeatedly implemented and challenged; all rejected.

- R7 is fully specified but unimplemented.
- Five major engine families are specified; none exists as a product implementation.
- Dirty Rust work is preseal research, not accepted source.
- All six PT locks remain open.
- No consumable post-1.0.0 uplift exists.

Under the current owner phase mark, parse-that execution remains paused after R6. R7 is the first technical implementation step once that execution boundary is reopened.

## BBNF

Substantial experiments exist, but none is a complete vertical:

- Type kernels v1–v3: implemented and falsified.
- Type kernel v4: hostile-falsified `INCOMPLETE_ASSAY`.
- JSON vertical A: implemented partial assay, rejected as a candidate.
- JSON oracle: extensive executable input oracle, but still RED and not product JSON.
- CSS L4 epochs 1–5: large corpus/input-assay work, but not CSS implementation; epoch 5 adjudicated 20/20 hostile findings correct.
- Graph P1/P2/P3: reproducible but falsified.
- P3.1: one narrow static representation reduction accepted; global movement remains RED.
- BB-C.W0 G3: unsound rule deletion retained as narrow static credit; executable replay failed before tests.
- BB-C.W0 ambient R2: implemented and frozen, but its build/test commands reached an unrelated `bbnf-path` linker SIGKILL before any test executed. It remains 0/2 pending two fresh hostile reviews and adjudication.
- VT-1…VT-4 directories do not exist.

Because `bbnf-lang` is expressly exempt from the cross-system phase pause, R2 review/adjudication and subsequent independently admitted BBNF work may continue.

## What remains

The ordered remainder is:

1. Two hostile reviews and adjudication of BB-C.W0 R2.
2. After parse execution authority resumes: implement and close PT-0 R7, then PT-0 W1–W3.
3. Execute T.W0–W7 and close `GESTALT-T`.
4. Execute U.W0–W9 and close both parse gestalts.
5. Complete the graph/DAG and Goldilocks modularization loop.
6. Freeze exhaustive JSON and CSS L4 conformance inputs and benchmark controls.
7. Implement four complete, independent every-lock VT candidates.
8. Derive exact Rust/TS types and the full value/tape API solely from BBNF modules.
9. Prove Rust/TS/Wasm/Apple-ARM backends, regex/bootstrap, CSP/egraph/cost, packages, and consumers.
10. Meet full-parity Lightning CSS and sonic-rs thresholds.
11. Close B1/B2, replay P1/P2, then Omega1/Omega2.
12. Only then land and visually verify the tertiary Glass UI 7 playground plane.

## Goal and cross-system coordination

The goal does need correction. Its objective is broadly correct, but its backend status remains incorrectly `blocked`. I attempted replacement twice; the goal service refused because it treats the blocked goal as unfinished, while exposing no edit/reactivate transition. I did not falsely mark unfinished work complete. The old goal must be cleared/reactivated by the product control before a replacement can be created.

- future crossings only through exact immutable path+hash asks.

The Glass message was delivered under the same rule. Value was not contacted, preserving its no-contact boundary. Thus Glass/SCI/Atlas do not block the near-orthogonal independent BBNF implementation.
